VIEW: DriftSpeed_PatchView
CREATE VIEW DriftSpeed_PatchView AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    slope_ppm,
    tick_vel_3s_up AS drift_speed,
    momentum_class,
    updated_at
FROM trend_features

VIEW: _orders_compat
CREATE VIEW _orders_compat AS
SELECT
    id,
    customerOrderRef,
    mode,
    run_id,
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    side,
    entry_odds,
    entry_stake,
    entry_status,
    entry_bet_id,
    opened_at,
    exit_status,
    exit_bet_id,
    exit_odds,
    exit_stake,
    closed_at,
    realized_pnl,
    net_pl,
    error,
    role,
    hedge_of,
    source,
    -- 🧩 unified missing columns
    NULL AS ts,
    NULL AS bf_bet_id,
    NULL AS customer_ref,
    COALESCE(entry_matched_odds, entry_odds) AS entry_matched_odds,
    COALESCE(entry_matched_stake, entry_stake) AS entry_matched_stake,
    COALESCE(exit_matched_odds, exit_odds) AS exit_matched_odds,
    COALESCE(exit_matched_stake, exit_stake) AS exit_matched_stake,
    NULL AS child_bf_bet_id,
    NULL AS notes,
    4 AS stop_ticks,
    0 AS stop_loss_triggered,
    0 AS greened_up,
    exit_kind
FROM orders

VIEW: bank_state
CREATE VIEW bank_state AS
 SELECT day,
        current_balance AS balance,
        start_balance,
        updated_at
 FROM internal_bank

VIEW: bias_summary
CREATE VIEW bias_summary AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    AVG(slope_ppm)       AS bias_value,
    MAX(momentum_class)  AS bias_dir,
    AVG(ABS(tick_vel_3s_up)) AS bias_conf
FROM v7_intelligence
WHERE slope_ppm IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY marketId, selectionId

VIEW: orders_effective
CREATE VIEW orders_effective AS
SELECT *
  FROM orders
 WHERE UPPER(COALESCE(entry_status,''))='PLACED'
   AND entry_bet_id IS NOT NULL

VIEW: runner_form_canonical
CREATE VIEW runner_form_canonical AS
SELECT
    selectionId,
    marketId,
    COALESCE(runs,0)   AS runs,
    COALESCE(wins,0)   AS wins,
    COALESCE(losses,0) AS losses,
    ROUND(COALESCE(net_pnl,0.0),3) AS avg_pnl,
    ROUND(
      CASE WHEN runs > 0 THEN CAST(wins AS REAL)/runs ELSE 0 END,
      3
    ) AS win_rate
FROM runner_history

VIEW: v7_cashout
CREATE VIEW v7_cashout AS
WITH live_orders AS (
    SELECT 
        marketId,
        selectionId,
        UPPER(side) AS side,
        COALESCE(entry_matched_odds, entry_odds) AS entry_odds,
        COALESCE(entry_matched_stake, entry_stake) AS stake
      FROM orders
     WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT')
       AND UPPER(entry_status)='MATCHED'
       AND (exit_status IS NULL OR exit_status='')
       AND date(opened_at)=date('now','utc')
),
latest_px AS (
    SELECT 
        o.marketId, 
        o.selectionId,
        COALESCE(oc.back1, oc.lay1, s.odd, 0.0) AS current_px
      FROM live_orders o
      LEFT JOIN odds_current oc USING(marketId,selectionId)
      LEFT JOIN oc_series s USING(marketId,selectionId)
),
runner_pnl AS (
    SELECT 
        o.marketId,
        o.selectionId,
        CASE 
            WHEN o.side='BACK' THEN (l.current_px - o.entry_odds) * o.stake
            WHEN o.side='LAY'  THEN (o.entry_odds - l.current_px) * o.stake
            ELSE 0.0
        END AS pnl_now
      FROM live_orders o
      LEFT JOIN latest_px l USING(marketId,selectionId)
),
market_agg AS (
    SELECT 
        marketId,
        ROUND(MAX(pnl_now),2) AS potential_cashout,
        ROUND(AVG(pnl_now),2) AS avg_cashout,
        ROUND(MIN(pnl_now),2) AS worst_cashout
      FROM runner_pnl
     GROUP BY marketId
)
SELECT 
    m.marketId,
    m.potential_cashout,
    m.avg_cashout,
    m.worst_cashout,
    (m.potential_cashout - ABS(m.worst_cashout)) AS spread_now,
    datetime('now','utc') AS snapshot_ts
FROM market_agg m

VIEW: v7_intelligence
CREATE VIEW v7_intelligence AS
    SELECT
        m.day,
        m.marketId,
        m.selectionId,
        m.letter,
        m.pnl,
        m.success,
        t.slope_ppm,
        t.tick_vel_3s_up,
        t.momentum_class,
        tf.drift_speed,
        tf.inplay_progress,
        tf.expected_race_mins,
        r.trainerName,
        r.jockeyName,
        r.distance,
        r.going
      FROM mastery_outcomes_raw m
      LEFT JOIN trend_features t
            USING (marketId,selectionId)
      LEFT JOIN v7_timing_features_fixed tf
            USING (marketId,selectionId)
      LEFT JOIN market_data r
            USING (marketId,selectionId)

VIEW: v7_intelligence_expanded
CREATE VIEW v7_intelligence_expanded AS
SELECT
    b.marketId,
    b.selectionId,
    b.letter,
    b.distance_band,
    b.fav_rank,
    b.class_band,
    t.drift_speed,
    t.inplay_progress,
    t.expected_race_mins,
    ROUND(t.inplay_avg_odds / t.pre_avg_odds, 3) AS drift_ratio,
    CASE
        WHEN t.inplay_avg_odds < t.pre_avg_odds * 0.8 THEN 'LEADING'
        WHEN t.inplay_avg_odds < t.pre_avg_odds * 1.1 THEN 'PROMINENT'
        WHEN t.inplay_avg_odds < t.pre_avg_odds * 1.4 THEN 'PRESSURED'
        ELSE 'REAR'
    END AS pos_inplay,
    p.pnl AS realized_pnl,
    CASE
        WHEN p.pnl > 0 THEN 'WIN'
        WHEN p.pnl < 0 THEN 'LOSS'
        ELSE 'NEUTRAL'
    END AS outcome
FROM v_timing_features_v7 t
JOIN v_mastery_v7 b
  USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN playbooks p
  USING (marketId, selectionId)

VIEW: v7_liability_risk
CREATE VIEW v7_liability_risk AS
WITH liab AS (
    SELECT
        marketId,
        selectionId,
        SUM(
            CASE
                WHEN UPPER(side)='LAY'
                    THEN ABS((entry_odds - 1.0) * entry_stake)
                ELSE ABS(entry_stake)
            END
        ) AS liability
    FROM orders
    WHERE role='PARENT'
      AND exit_status IN ('open','pending','active')   -- ✅ only live orders
    GROUP BY marketId, selectionId
),
conf AS (
    SELECT
        SUBSTR(bin_key,1,INSTR(bin_key,'|')-1) AS marketId,
        SUBSTR(bin_key,INSTR(bin_key,'|')+1)   AS selectionId,
        ROUND(confidence*100.0,2) AS win_pct
    FROM mastery_posteriors
),
base AS (
    SELECT c.marketId,c.selectionId,c.win_pct FROM conf c
    UNION
    SELECT marketId,selectionId,ROUND(AVG(success)*100.0,2) AS win_pct
      FROM cache_mastery_outcomes
     GROUP BY marketId,selectionId
)
SELECT
    liab.marketId,
    liab.selectionId,
    ROUND(liab.liability,2) AS liability,
    COALESCE(b.win_pct,0.0) AS win_pct
FROM liab
LEFT JOIN base b
  ON liab.marketId=b.marketId
 AND liab.selectionId=b.selectionId

VIEW: v7_mastery_race_summary_new
CREATE VIEW v7_mastery_race_summary_new AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId) AS runners,
    ROUND(AVG(drift_speed),4) AS avg_drift_speed,
    ROUND(AVG(inplay_progress),4) AS avg_progress,
    ROUND(AVG(expected_race_mins),2) AS avg_expected_mins,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)*1.0/COUNT(*),3) AS win_rate_est,
    datetime('now','utc') AS created_at
FROM v7_mastery_race_view_new
GROUP BY marketId

VIEW: v7_oc_drift_unfolded
CREATE VIEW v7_oc_drift_unfolded AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    anchor_odd,
    oc_idx,
    oc_val,
    ROUND(oc_val / anchor_odd, 3) AS drift_ratio
FROM (
    SELECT
        marketId,
        selectionId,
        anchor_odd,
        1  AS oc_idx, oc1  AS oc_val FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc1  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 2, oc2  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc2  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 3, oc3  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc3  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 4, oc4  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc4  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 5, oc5  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc5  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 6, oc6  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc6  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 7, oc7  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc7  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 8, oc8  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc8  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 9, oc9  FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc9  IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 10, oc10 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc10 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 11, oc11 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc11 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 12, oc12 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc12 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 13, oc13 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc13 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 14, oc14 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc14 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 15, oc15 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc15 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 16, oc16 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc16 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 17, oc17 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc17 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 18, oc18 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc18 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 19, oc19 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc19 IS NOT NULL
    UNION ALL SELECT marketId, selectionId, anchor_odd, 20, oc20 FROM inbound_oc_cache WHERE oc20 IS NOT NULL
)

VIEW: v7_preoff_position
CREATE VIEW v7_preoff_position AS
SELECT
    t.marketId,
    t.selectionId,
    t.anchor_odd,
    tf.pre_avg_odds,
    (tf.pre_avg_odds - t.anchor_odd)/t.anchor_odd AS drift_ratio,
    CASE
        WHEN (tf.pre_avg_odds - t.anchor_odd)/t.anchor_odd < -0.10 THEN 'STEAMER'
        WHEN (tf.pre_avg_odds - t.anchor_odd)/t.anchor_odd >  0.10 THEN 'DRIFTER'
        ELSE 'FLAT'
    END AS pre_shape,
    CASE
        WHEN tf.pre_avg_odds <= 2.0  THEN 'FRONT'
        WHEN tf.pre_avg_odds <= 4.0  THEN 'CONTENDER'
        WHEN tf.pre_avg_odds <= 8.0  THEN 'MID'
        WHEN tf.pre_avg_odds <= 16.0 THEN 'OUTSIDER'
        ELSE 'LONG'
    END AS odds_band
FROM tmp_anchor_snapshot AS t
LEFT JOIN v_timing_features_v7 AS tf
  USING (marketId, selectionId)
WHERE tf.pre_avg_odds IS NOT NULL

VIEW: v7_race_position_final
CREATE VIEW v7_race_position_final AS
SELECT
    b.event_name AS venue,
    b.market_name AS race_name,
    rf.runner_name AS horse_name,
    b.anchor_odd,
    ROUND((ioc.oc10 / b.anchor_odd) - 1.0, 3) AS drift_50,
    CASE
        WHEN (ioc.oc10 / b.anchor_odd) < 0.8 THEN 'LEADING'
        WHEN (ioc.oc10 / b.anchor_odd) < 1.0 THEN 'PROMINENT'
        WHEN (ioc.oc10 / b.anchor_odd) < 1.3 THEN 'PRESSURED'
        ELSE 'REAR'
    END AS pos_50,
    CASE
        WHEN rf.profit > 0 THEN 'WIN'
        WHEN rf.profit < 0 THEN 'LOSS'
        ELSE 'NEUTRAL'
    END AS outcome
FROM inbound_oc_cache ioc
JOIN bets_snapshot b USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN runner_form_snapshot rf USING (marketId, selectionId)
WHERE b.anchor_odd IS NOT NULL

VIEW: v7_race_position_inferred
CREATE VIEW v7_race_position_inferred AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    anchor_odd,
    oc_idx,
    oc_val,
    ROUND(drift_ratio,3) AS drift_ratio,
    CASE
        WHEN drift_ratio < 0.8 THEN 'LEADING'
        WHEN drift_ratio < 1.1 THEN 'PROMINENT'
        WHEN drift_ratio < 2.5 THEN 'PRESSURED'
        ELSE 'REAR'
    END AS pos_inplay
FROM v7_oc_drift_unfolded

VIEW: v7_race_position_named
CREATE VIEW v7_race_position_named AS
SELECT
    m.day,
    m.marketId,
    m.selectionId,
    m.venue,
    m.race_name,
    m.horse_name,
    m.anchor_odd AS anchor,
    ROUND((ioc.oc10 / m.anchor_odd) - 1.0, 3) AS drift_50,
    CASE
      WHEN (ioc.oc10 / m.anchor_odd) < 0.9 THEN 'FRONT'
      WHEN (ioc.oc10 / m.anchor_odd) < 1.1 THEN 'MID'
      ELSE 'REAR'
    END AS pos_50,
    CASE
      WHEN pb.pnl > 0 THEN 'WIN'
      WHEN pb.pnl < 0 THEN 'LOSS'
      ELSE 'NEUTRAL'
    END AS outcome
FROM bets_meta_cache m
LEFT JOIN inbound_oc_cache ioc
       ON ioc.marketId = m.marketId
      AND ioc.selectionId = m.selectionId
LEFT JOIN playbooks pb
       ON pb.marketId = m.marketId
      AND pb.selectionId = m.selectionId

VIEW: v7_race_position_summary
CREATE VIEW v7_race_position_summary AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    anchor_odd,
    MAX(CASE WHEN oc_idx=1  THEN pos_inplay END)  AS pos_1,
    MAX(CASE WHEN oc_idx=5  THEN pos_inplay END)  AS pos_5,
    MAX(CASE WHEN oc_idx=10 THEN pos_inplay END)  AS pos_10,
    MAX(CASE WHEN oc_idx=15 THEN pos_inplay END)  AS pos_15,
    MAX(CASE WHEN oc_idx=19 THEN pos_inplay END)  AS pos_19,
    ROUND(AVG(drift_ratio),3)                    AS avg_drift,
    CASE
        WHEN AVG(drift_ratio) < 0.9 THEN 'HELD_FORM'
        WHEN AVG(drift_ratio) < 1.2 THEN 'FADED'
        WHEN AVG(drift_ratio) < 2.0 THEN 'PRESSURED'
        ELSE 'BLEW_OUT'
    END AS race_pattern
FROM v7_race_position_inferred
GROUP BY marketId, selectionId

VIEW: v7_shape_summary
CREATE VIEW v7_shape_summary AS
    SELECT
        marketId,
        selectionId,
        AVG(drift_speed) AS avg_drift,
        MAX(drift_speed) AS drift_speed,
        MAX(inplay_progress) AS inplay_progress,
        MAX(expected_race_mins) AS expected_race_mins,
        CASE
          WHEN MAX(drift_speed) > 0.15 THEN 'FADE'
          WHEN MIN(drift_speed) < -0.10 THEN 'CLOSE_FINISH'
          ELSE 'STEADY'
        END AS race_pattern
      FROM v7_timing_features_fixed
     GROUP BY marketId, selectionId

VIEW: v7_timing_features_fixed
CREATE VIEW v7_timing_features_fixed AS
    SELECT
        marketId, selectionId,
        pre_avg_odds, inplay_avg_odds, post_avg_odds,
        drift_speed, inplay_progress, expected_race_mins
      FROM v_timing_features_v7

VIEW: v_blueprint_playbook_train
CREATE VIEW v_blueprint_playbook_train AS
SELECT
    bp.day,
    bp.surface_key,
    bp.pattern_key,
    bp.runner_name,
    bp.marketId,
    bp.selectionId,
    bp.move,
    bp.trade_type,
    bp.confidence AS blueprint_confidence,
    bp.entry       AS blueprint_entry,
    bp.exit        AS blueprint_exit,
    bp.trend_ticks,
    tb.net_pl,
    tb.trade_result,
    tb.outcome_bin,
    mq.match_quality,
    mq.event_count,
    tb.market_win_rate,
    tb.avg_market_pnl,
    tb.profit_rate
FROM blueprint_flat bp
LEFT JOIN v_mastery_training_base tb
       ON CAST(bp.marketId AS TEXT)=CAST(tb.marketId AS TEXT)
      AND CAST(bp.selectionId AS TEXT)=CAST(tb.selectionId AS TEXT)
LEFT JOIN v_match_metrics mq
       ON CAST(bp.marketId AS TEXT)=CAST(mq.marketId AS TEXT)
      AND (CAST(bp.selectionId AS TEXT)=CAST(mq.selectionId AS TEXT)
           OR mq.selectionId IS NULL)

VIEW: v_dashboard_cashout
CREATE VIEW v_dashboard_cashout AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    pnl_now      AS total,
    liability    AS liability,
    status,
    updated_at
FROM live_state
WHERE status='OPEN'

VIEW: v_dashboard_liabilities
CREATE VIEW v_dashboard_liabilities AS
SELECT
    c.marketId,
    ROUND(SUM(c.liability),2) AS liability,
    COUNT(*) AS markets
FROM v_dashboard_cashout AS c
GROUP BY c.marketId
HAVING liability > 0

VIEW: v_fav_rank_form
CREATE VIEW v_fav_rank_form AS
WITH enriched AS (
  SELECT
      r.fav_rank_bin,
      CASE
          WHEN ra.anchor_odds <= 1.5 THEN 'under_1_5'
          WHEN ra.anchor_odds <= 3   THEN '1_5_to_3'
          WHEN ra.anchor_odds <= 5   THEN '3_to_5'
          WHEN ra.anchor_odds <= 7   THEN '5_to_7'
          ELSE '7_plus'
      END AS odds_band,
      ra.drift_ratio,
      ps.outcome_bin,
      ra.anchor_odds,
      ra.off_odds
  FROM playbooks_settled ps
  JOIN v_rank_bin r
       ON ps.marketId = r.marketId
      AND ps.selectionId = r.selectionId
  JOIN v_runner_anchor_off ra
       ON ps.marketId = ra.marketId
      AND ps.selectionId = ra.selectionId
  WHERE r.fav_rank_bin IN ('1','2','3','4','5','6')
)
SELECT
    fav_rank_bin,
    odds_band,
    COUNT(*)                           AS runs,
    SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS wins,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN' THEN 1.0 ELSE 0 END)/COUNT(*),3) AS win_rate,
    ROUND(AVG(drift_ratio),3)          AS avg_drift,
    ROUND(AVG(anchor_odds),2)          AS avg_anchor_odds,
    ROUND(AVG(off_odds),2)             AS avg_off_odds
FROM enriched
GROUP BY fav_rank_bin, odds_band
ORDER BY fav_rank_bin, odds_band

VIEW: v_liability_context
CREATE VIEW v_liability_context AS
SELECT 
    m.id AS id,            -- expose internal id for checkpointing
    m.day,
    m.marketId,
    m.selectionId,
    m.letter,
    m.distance_band,
    m.fav_rank,
    m.day_of_week,
    m.surface,
    m.class_band,
    m.target_ticks,
    m.realized_ticks,
    m.success,
    m.stoploss_hit,
    m.hedge_hit,
    m.entry_px,
    m.pnl,
    m.weight,
    m.priority,
    COALESCE(ls.liability, 0.0) AS liab_value,
    CASE WHEN ls.liability > 250.0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS liab_over250
FROM mastery_outcomes_raw AS m
LEFT JOIN liability_signals AS ls
  ON m.marketId = ls.marketId
 AND m.selectionId = ls.selectionId
 AND m.day = ls.day

VIEW: v_market_context
CREATE VIEW v_market_context AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId) AS runners,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) * 1.0 /
          COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId), 3) AS market_win_rate,
    ROUND(AVG(net_pl), 2) AS avg_market_pnl,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN net_pl > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) * 1.0 /
          COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId), 3) AS profit_rate
FROM playbooks_settled
GROUP BY marketId

VIEW: v_market_sentiment
CREATE VIEW v_market_sentiment AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    CASE
        WHEN momentum_class='STEAM' THEN 1.0
        WHEN momentum_class='DRIFT' THEN -1.0
        ELSE 0.0
    END AS sentiment,
    ROUND(slope_ppm * 100.0, 4) AS drift_pct
FROM trend_features
WHERE slope_ppm IS NOT NULL

VIEW: v_mastery_brain_global
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_brain_global AS
SELECT
    COUNT(*) AS samples,
    ROUND(AVG(confidence),4) AS p1_mean,
    ROUND(AVG(bucket_confidence),4) AS p2_mean,
    ROUND(AVG(live_pnl_ratio),4) AS p3_mean,
    ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(weight, weight_applied, 1.0)),4) AS weight,
    ROUND(SUM(live_pnl_ratio),4) AS total_pnl
FROM mastery_posteriors

VIEW: v_mastery_brain_input
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_brain_input AS
WITH
pnl AS (
    SELECT marketId, selectionId,
           ROUND(SUM(net_pl),4) AS pnl_total
      FROM playbooks_settled
     GROUP BY marketId, selectionId
),
cash AS (
    SELECT marketId,
           potential_cashout AS cashout_total,
           worst_cashout,
           spread_now
      FROM v7_cashout
),
poster AS (
    SELECT
        SUBSTR(bin_key,1,INSTR(bin_key,'|')-1) AS marketId,
        SUBSTR(bin_key,INSTR(bin_key,'|')+1)   AS selectionId,
        confidence,
        bucket_confidence
      FROM mastery_posteriors
)
SELECT
    mq.marketId,
    mq.selectionId,
    mq.match_quality_score,
    mq.good_match,
    mq.bad_match,
    p.pnl_total,
    c.cashout_total,
    c.worst_cashout,
    c.spread_now,
    mp.confidence,
    mp.bucket_confidence,
    ROUND(COALESCE(p.pnl_total,0),3) AS pnl_now,
    ROUND(COALESCE(c.cashout_total,0),3) AS pnl_potential,
    ROUND(COALESCE(mq.match_quality_score,0),3) AS match_now,
    ROUND(COALESCE(mp.confidence,0),3) AS conf_now,
    ROUND(COALESCE(mp.bucket_confidence,0),3) AS bucket_now,
    CASE
        WHEN mq.good_match=1 AND p.pnl_total>0 THEN 'STRONG'
        WHEN mq.good_match=1 AND p.pnl_total<=0 THEN 'VOLATILE'
        WHEN mq.bad_match=1 THEN 'RISK'
        ELSE 'NEUTRAL'
    END AS brain_state_label
FROM v_mastery_match_quality mq
LEFT JOIN pnl p USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN cash c USING (marketId)
LEFT JOIN poster mp USING (marketId, selectionId)

VIEW: v_mastery_brain_macro
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_brain_macro AS
        SELECT
            bucket,
            COUNT(*) AS samples,
            ROUND(AVG(confidence),4) AS p1_mean,
            ROUND(AVG(bucket_confidence),4) AS p2_mean,
            ROUND(AVG(live_pnl_ratio),4) AS p3_mean,
            ROUND(AVG(COALESCE(weight, weight_applied, 1.0)),4) AS weight
        FROM mastery_posteriors
        GROUP BY bucket

VIEW: v_mastery_core_results
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_core_results AS
SELECT
    day,
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    net_pl,
    CASE WHEN net_pl > 0 THEN 'PROFIT' ELSE 'LOSS' END AS trade_result,
    outcome_bin
FROM playbooks_settled

VIEW: v_mastery_intel_v7
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_intel_v7 AS
SELECT
    marketId, selectionId, day, letter,
    pnl, success, anchor_odd, pre_drift,
    slope_ppm, tick_vel_3s_up, momentum_class,
    expected_race_mins, drift_speed, inplay_progress,
    pre_vol, inplay_vol, post_vol,
    pre_vol AS avg_volatility,
    inplay_vol AS band_rel_vol,
    post_vol AS band_stability,
    expected_race_mins AS mto_minutes,
    win_rate AS segment_form_win_rate,
    pre_vol AS autoavg_volatility,
    slope_ppm AS drift_pct,
    pre_drift AS plan_drift_pct,
    drift_speed AS actual_drift_pct,
    momentum_class='REVERSAL' AS reversal_flag,
    0.5 AS threshold,
    success AS matched_ratio,
    1.0 AS weight,
    0.0 AS totalMatched,
    0.0 AS hedge_eff,
    win_rate AS fav_rank_entry,
    form_avg_pnl, going, distance,
    trainer AS trainerName, jockey AS jockeyName,
    class_band, pattern_key, surface_key, trade_type,
    'LIVE' AS mode
FROM v_mastery_training_input

VIEW: v_mastery_intelligence_runner
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_intelligence_runner AS
SELECT
    pb.day,
    pb.marketId,
    pb.selectionId,
    pb.strategy,
    pb.oc_stage,
    pb.entry_odds,
    pb.exit_odds,
    pb.pnl,
    pb.outcome,
    pb.confidence,
    tf.slope_ppm,
    tf.tick_vel_3s_up,
    tf.momentum_class,
    s.sentiment,
    s.drift_pct,
    w.wom_ratio,
    w.wom_state,
    f.runs,
    f.wins,
    f.win_rate,
    f.avg_pnl,
    t.expected_race_mins,
    t.inplay_progress,
    t.drift_speed,
    ROUND((pb.exit_odds - pb.entry_odds)/NULLIF(pb.entry_odds,0),4) AS odds_change_pct,
    CASE
        WHEN (pb.exit_odds - pb.entry_odds)/NULLIF(pb.entry_odds,0) < -0.2 THEN 'Large_Steam'
        WHEN ABS((pb.exit_odds - pb.entry_odds)/NULLIF(pb.entry_odds,0)) <= 0.2 THEN 'Flat'
        ELSE 'Drift'
    END AS odds_change_bin,
    CASE WHEN pb.pnl > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS win_flag,
    'LIVE' AS mode
FROM playbooks pb
LEFT JOIN trend_features        AS tf USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN v_market_sentiment    AS s  USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN v_weight_of_money     AS w  USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN runner_form_canonical AS f  USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN v_timing_features_v7  AS t  USING (marketId, selectionId)

VIEW: v_mastery_live
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_live AS
SELECT
    distance_band,
    fav_rank_bin,
    surface_type,
    day_of_week,
    COUNT(*)            AS bins,
    ROUND(SUM(net_pnl), 2) AS total_pnl,
    ROUND(AVG(mae_mean), 3) AS avg_mae,
    ROUND(SUM(weight_applied), 1) AS total_weight,
    ROUND(SUM(p1_alpha) / NULLIF(SUM(p1_alpha + p1_beta), 0), 3) AS p1_mean,
    ROUND(SUM(p2_alpha) / NULLIF(SUM(p2_alpha + p2_beta), 0), 3) AS p2_mean,
    ROUND(SUM(p3_alpha) / NULLIF(SUM(p3_alpha + p3_beta), 0), 3) AS p3_mean
FROM mastery_posteriors
WHERE source IN ('PLAYBOOKS_LIVE', 'PLAYBOOKS')
GROUP BY distance_band, fav_rank_bin, surface_type, day_of_week

VIEW: v_mastery_match_quality
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_match_quality AS
WITH
orders_base AS (
  SELECT
      marketId,
      selectionId,
      SUM(CASE WHEN role='PARENT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS parents,
      SUM(CASE WHEN role='CHILD' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS children,
      SUM(CASE WHEN role='PARENT' AND entry_status='matched' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS parents_matched,
      SUM(CASE WHEN role='CHILD'  AND entry_status='matched' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS children_matched,
      SUM(CASE WHEN stop_loss_triggered=1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS stoploss_hits
    FROM orders
   WHERE date(opened_at) >= date('now','-90 day')
   GROUP BY marketId, selectionId
),
playbook_pnl AS (
  SELECT marketId, selectionId,
         SUM(net_pl) AS total_pnl
    FROM playbooks_settled
   GROUP BY marketId, selectionId
)
SELECT
    o.marketId,
    o.selectionId,
    o.parents,
    o.children,
    o.parents_matched,
    o.children_matched,
    o.stoploss_hits,
    CASE WHEN o.parents_matched>0 AND o.parents_matched=o.children_matched THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS full_match_flag,
    CASE WHEN o.stoploss_hits>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS stop_loss_triggered,
    CASE WHEN o.parents_matched>0 AND o.parents_matched=o.children_matched AND o.stoploss_hits=0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS good_match,
    CASE WHEN o.stoploss_hits>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS bad_match,
    CASE WHEN COALESCE(p.total_pnl,0)>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS profit_flag,
    ROUND(COALESCE(p.total_pnl,0),2) AS total_pnl,
    ROUND(CAST(o.parents_matched AS REAL)/NULLIF(o.parents,0),3) AS confidence_proxy,
    ROUND(
      (
        (CASE WHEN o.parents_matched>0 AND o.parents_matched=o.children_matched AND o.stoploss_hits=0 THEN 1.0 ELSE 0.0 END)
        - (CASE WHEN o.stoploss_hits>0 THEN 0.5 ELSE 0.0 END)
      ) * CAST(o.parents_matched AS REAL)/NULLIF(o.parents,1),
      3
    ) AS match_quality_score
FROM orders_base o
LEFT JOIN playbook_pnl p USING (marketId,selectionId)

VIEW: v_mastery_pattern_train
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_pattern_train AS
SELECT
    core.day,
    core.marketId,
    core.selectionId,
    core.net_pl,
    core.trade_result,
    core.outcome_bin,
    z.odds_band,
    z.win_rate,
    tg.trading_zone,
    rg.form_class
FROM v_mastery_core_results core
LEFT JOIN dashboard_runners dr
       ON core.marketId = dr.marketId
      AND CAST(core.selectionId AS TEXT)=dr.selectionId
LEFT JOIN v_runner_context_form z
       ON dr.runner_name = z.runner_name
LEFT JOIN v_trading_zone_groups tg
       ON z.odds_band = tg.odds_band
LEFT JOIN v_runner_form_groups rg
       ON z.odds_band = rg.odds_band

VIEW: v_mastery_training
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_training AS
WITH
  cash AS (
    SELECT 
        marketId,
        MAX(CAST(json_extract(json_payload,'$.cashout_total') AS REAL))   AS cashout_total,
        MAX(CAST(json_extract(json_payload,'$.liability_total') AS REAL)) AS liability_total
      FROM mastery_cache
     WHERE event_type='cashout_tick'
     GROUP BY marketId
  ),
  fb AS (
    SELECT 
        marketId,
        COALESCE(AVG(pnl_now),0) AS avg_pnl_now,
        COALESCE(AVG(bias_delta),0) AS avg_bias_delta
      FROM mastery_feedback
     GROUP BY marketId
  ),
  ms AS (
    SELECT 
        marketId,
        COALESCE((julianday(off_at_utc) - julianday('now'))*1440, 0) AS mto_minutes
      FROM markets_schedule
  )
SELECT
    COALESCE(fb.marketId, cash.marketId, ms.marketId) AS marketId,
    COALESCE(cash.cashout_total,0)    AS pnl_total,
    COALESCE(cash.liability_total,0)  AS liability_total,
    ROUND(COALESCE(cash.cashout_total / NULLIF(cash.liability_total,0),0),5) AS ratio,
    COALESCE(fb.avg_bias_delta,0)     AS slope_ppm,
    0.0                               AS tick_vel_3s_up,
    COALESCE(ms.mto_minutes,0)        AS mto_minutes,
    'unknown'                         AS action,
    COALESCE(fb.avg_pnl_now,0)        AS reward
FROM fb
LEFT JOIN cash ON fb.marketId = cash.marketId
LEFT JOIN ms   ON fb.marketId = ms.marketId

VIEW: v_mastery_training_base
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_training_base AS
SELECT
    r.day,
    r.marketId,
    r.selectionId,
    r.net_pl,
    r.trade_result,
    r.outcome_bin,
    m.runners            AS market_runners,
    m.market_win_rate,
    m.avg_market_pnl,
    m.profit_rate
FROM v_runner_truth r
LEFT JOIN v_market_context m USING (marketId)

VIEW: v_mastery_training_input
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_training_input AS
SELECT
    i.marketId,
    i.selectionId,
    date(i.last_sync_ts) AS day,
    p.letter,
    p.net_pl        AS pnl,
    CASE WHEN p.net_pl>0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS success,
    i.anchor_odd,
    ((i.oc1 - i.anchor_odd)/NULLIF(i.anchor_odd,0)) AS pre_drift,
    tf.slope_ppm,
    tf.tick_vel_3s_up,
    tf.momentum_class,
    t.expected_race_mins,
    t.drift_speed,
    t.inplay_progress,
    t.pre_vol,
    t.inplay_vol,
    t.post_vol,
    r.win_rate,
    r.avg_pnl           AS form_avg_pnl,
    p.going,
    p.distance,
    p.trainer,
    p.jockey,
    p.class_band,
    b.pattern_key,
    b.surface_key,
    b.trade_type,

    -- 🧩 Reintroduced bucket mapping
    CASE
      WHEN p.letter IN ('A','S','B') THEN 'Market Conditions'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('G','X','R') THEN 'Timing'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('F','L') THEN 'Bias / Drift'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('T','P') THEN 'Execution'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('M','Z') THEN 'Blueprint Recall'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('V','J') THEN 'Venue / Type / Going'
      WHEN p.letter IN ('H','D','R','S','M') THEN 'Risk / Hedge'
      ELSE 'Stability / Goal Alignment'
    END AS bucket

FROM inbound_oc_cache        AS i
LEFT JOIN playbooks_settled  AS p  USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN trend_features     AS tf USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN v_timing_features_v7 AS t USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN runner_form_canonical AS r USING (marketId, selectionId)
LEFT JOIN blueprint_flat     AS b  USING (marketId, selectionId)
WHERE i.last_sync_ts IS NOT NULL

VIEW: v_mastery_training_unified
CREATE VIEW v_mastery_training_unified AS
SELECT
    m.day,
    m.marketId,
    m.selectionId,
    m.letter,
    m.pnl,
    m.success,
    m.pre_drift,
    m.band_width,       -- ✅ correct live column name
    m.band_rel_vol,
    m.band_bias,
    m.band_skew,
    m.band_stability,
    m.slope_ppm,
    m.tick_vel_3s_up,
    m.momentum_class,
    m.expected_race_mins,
    m.drift_speed,
    m.inplay_progress,
    m.win_rate,
    m.form_avg_pnl,
    f.runner_name,
    f.runs AS form_runs,
    f.wins AS form_wins,
    f.win_rate AS form_win_rate,
    f.avg_pnl AS form_avg_pnl_2
FROM cache_mastery_outcomes AS m
LEFT JOIN runner_form_canonical AS f
  USING (marketId, selectionId)

VIEW: v_mastery_v7

        SELECT *
          FROM mastery_outcomes_raw
         WHERE date(day) >= date('now','-90 day')

VIEW: v_match_metrics
CREATE VIEW v_match_metrics AS
WITH parsed AS (
  SELECT
    ts,
    json_extract(substr(message,9), '$.type')        AS event_type,
    json_extract(substr(message,9), '$.marketId')    AS marketId,
    json_extract(substr(message,9), '$.selectionId') AS selectionId
  FROM events
  WHERE message LIKE 'MASTERY %'
)
SELECT
  marketId,
  selectionId,
  ROUND(AVG(
        CASE
          WHEN event_type IN ('greenup','micro_lay','hedge') THEN 1.0
          WHEN event_type IN ('feedback_tick','cashout_tick') THEN 0.8
          WHEN event_type IN ('market_risk','feedback_summary','feedback_assimilated','feedback_assimilated_summary') THEN 0.7
          WHEN event_type IN ('liability_signal') THEN 0.4
          WHEN event_type IN ('mlm_cap_hit') THEN 0.3
          WHEN event_type IN ('stop_loss_breached','stoploss') THEN 0.0
          ELSE NULL
        END
  ),3) AS match_quality,
  COUNT(*) AS event_count
FROM parsed
WHERE marketId IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY marketId, selectionId

VIEW: v_oc_timing
CREATE VIEW v_oc_timing AS
SELECT
    oc.marketId,
    oc.selectionId,
    oc.stage,
    oc.snapshot_ts,
    rt.surface_type,
    rt.expected_seconds,
    ROUND((julianday(oc.snapshot_ts) - julianday(rt.off_time_utc)) * 24 * 60, 2) AS delta_minutes,
    ROUND(
      ((julianday(oc.snapshot_ts) - julianday(rt.off_time_utc)) * 24 * 60 * 60)
      / rt.expected_seconds,
      4
    ) AS progress_ratio,
    CASE
      WHEN ((julianday(oc.snapshot_ts) - julianday(rt.off_time_utc)) * 24 * 60) < 0 THEN 'PRE'
      WHEN ((julianday(oc.snapshot_ts) - julianday(rt.off_time_utc)) * 24 * 60)
           BETWEEN 0 AND (rt.expected_seconds / 60) THEN 'INPLAY'
      WHEN ((julianday(oc.snapshot_ts) - julianday(rt.off_time_utc)) * 24 * 60)
           > (rt.expected_seconds / 60) THEN 'POST'
      ELSE 'UNKNOWN'
    END AS phase
FROM oc_series oc
JOIN v_race_timing rt
  ON oc.marketId = rt.marketId
WHERE oc.snapshot_ts IS NOT NULL
  AND rt.expected_seconds IS NOT NULL
  AND (julianday(rt.off_time_utc) - julianday(oc.snapshot_ts)) * 24 * 60 BETWEEN -240 AND 30
GROUP BY oc.marketId, oc.stage, oc.selectionId

VIEW: v_odds_first_last_today
CREATE VIEW v_odds_first_last_today AS
WITH raw AS (
  SELECT marketId, selectionId, snapshot_ts, odd, stage, id,
         CASE WHEN stage GLOB 'OC[0-9]*' THEN CAST(SUBSTR(stage,3) AS INT) ELSE 0 END AS stage_n
  FROM oc_series
  WHERE date(snapshot_ts)=date('now','utc')
),
first_ts AS ( SELECT marketId, selectionId, MIN(snapshot_ts) AS first_ts FROM raw GROUP BY marketId, selectionId ),
last_ts  AS ( SELECT marketId, selectionId, MAX(snapshot_ts) AS last_ts  FROM raw GROUP BY marketId, selectionId ),
first_pick AS (
  SELECT r.marketId, r.selectionId, r.snapshot_ts AS ts, r.odd AS ltp,
         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY r.marketId, r.selectionId, r.snapshot_ts ORDER BY r.stage_n DESC, r.id DESC) AS rn
  FROM raw r JOIN first_ts f ON r.marketId=f.marketId AND r.selectionId=f.selectionId AND r.snapshot_ts=f.first_ts
),
last_pick AS (
  SELECT r.marketId, r.selectionId, r.snapshot_ts AS ts, r.odd AS ltp,
         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY r.marketId, r.selectionId, r.snapshot_ts ORDER BY r.stage_n DESC, r.id DESC) AS rn
  FROM raw r JOIN last_ts l ON r.marketId=l.marketId AND r.selectionId=l.selectionId AND r.snapshot_ts=l.last_ts
)
SELECT f.marketId, f.selectionId, CAST(f.ltp AS REAL) AS ltp_first, CAST(l.ltp AS REAL) AS ltp_last
FROM first_pick f JOIN last_pick l ON f.marketId=l.marketId AND f.selectionId=l.selectionId
WHERE f.rn=1 AND l.rn=1

VIEW: v_odds_latest
CREATE VIEW v_odds_latest AS
WITH latest AS (
  SELECT marketId, selectionId, MAX(snapshot_ts) AS max_ts
  FROM oc_series
  GROUP BY marketId, selectionId
),
stage_ordered AS (
  SELECT o.*,
         CASE WHEN o.stage GLOB 'OC[0-9]*' THEN CAST(SUBSTR(o.stage,3) AS INT) ELSE 0 END AS stage_n
  FROM oc_series o
  JOIN latest l
    ON o.marketId=l.marketId AND o.selectionId=l.selectionId AND o.snapshot_ts=l.max_ts
),
one_per_sid AS (
  SELECT marketId, selectionId, odd AS ltp, snapshot_ts,
         ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
            PARTITION BY marketId, selectionId, snapshot_ts
            ORDER BY stage_n DESC, id DESC
         ) AS rn
  FROM stage_ordered
)
SELECT marketId, selectionId, CAST(ltp AS REAL) AS ltp, snapshot_ts
FROM one_per_sid
WHERE rn=1

VIEW: v_order_settlements
CREATE VIEW v_order_settlements AS
SELECT
    o.id              AS order_id,
    o.marketId        AS marketId,
    o.selectionId     AS selectionId,
    o.side            AS side,
    o.entry_odds      AS entry_odds,
    o.entry_stake     AS entry_stake,
    o.exit_status     AS exit_status,
    o.closed_at       AS closed_at,
    b.betId           AS betId,
    b.profit          AS settle_profit,
    b.commission      AS settle_commission,
    b.settledDate     AS settled_at
FROM orders o
LEFT JOIN bf_cleared_orders_local b
     ON o.bf_bet_id = b.betId
    AND o.marketId  = b.marketId
    AND o.selectionId = b.selectionId

VIEW: v_orders_v7
CREATE VIEW v_orders_v7 AS SELECT * FROM orders

VIEW: v_phase_performance
CREATE VIEW v_phase_performance AS
SELECT
    v.marketId,
    v.selectionId,
    v.surface_type,
    v.phase,
    COUNT(*)                         AS n_points,
    MIN(v.delta_minutes)             AS phase_start_min,
    MAX(v.delta_minutes)             AS phase_end_min,
    ROUND(AVG(v.progress_ratio), 4)  AS avg_progress_ratio,
    ROUND(
      AVG(CASE WHEN oc.odd IS NOT NULL THEN oc.odd END),
      4
    ) AS avg_odds,
    ROUND(
      (MAX(oc.odd) - MIN(oc.odd)) / NULLIF(AVG(oc.odd),0),
      4
    ) AS rel_volatility,
    ROUND(
      (MAX(oc.odd) - MIN(oc.odd)) / 
      NULLIF(MAX(v.delta_minutes) - MIN(v.delta_minutes),0),
      4
    ) AS odds_rate_per_min
FROM v_oc_timing v
JOIN oc_series oc
  ON v.marketId=oc.marketId AND v.selectionId=oc.selectionId AND v.stage=oc.stage
WHERE v.phase IN ('PRE','INPLAY','POST')
GROUP BY v.marketId, v.selectionId, v.phase

VIEW: v_phase_summary_market
CREATE VIEW v_phase_summary_market AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    surface_type,
    phase,
    COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId) AS n_runners,
    ROUND(AVG(rel_volatility),4) AS avg_vol,
    ROUND(MAX(rel_volatility),4) AS max_vol,
    ROUND(AVG(odds_rate_per_min),4) AS avg_rate,
    ROUND(MAX(odds_rate_per_min),4) AS max_rate,
    (
      SELECT selectionId
      FROM v_phase_performance v2
      WHERE v2.marketId=v1.marketId AND v2.phase=v1.phase
      ORDER BY v2.rel_volatility DESC LIMIT 1
    ) AS most_volatile_runner
FROM v_phase_performance v1
GROUP BY marketId, phase

VIEW: v_plan_ledger_compat
CREATE VIEW v_plan_ledger_compat AS
SELECT
    id,
    day,
    run_id,
    plan_id,
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    letter,
    note,
    scalp_direction AS direction,
    proposed_odds   AS px,
    proposed_stake  AS size,
    confidence,
    blueprint_match,
    window_tag,
    status,
    why,
    parent_order_id,
    child_order_id,
    epic_id,
    epic_stories,
    epic_rank,
    decided_at,
    updated_at
FROM plan_ledger

VIEW: v_playbooks_all
CREATE VIEW v_playbooks_all AS
SELECT
    class_band,
    confidence,
    created_at,
    day,
    entry_odds,
    entry_stake,
    exit_odds,
    exit_stake,
    letter,
    marketId,
    meta_json,
    net_pl,
    oc_stage_entry,
    selectionId,
    side,
    strategy
FROM playbooks_settled
UNION ALL
SELECT
    class_band,
    confidence,
    created_at,
    day,
    entry_odds,
    entry_stake,
    exit_odds,
    exit_stake,
    letter,
    marketId,
    meta_json,
    net_pl,
    oc_stage_entry,
    selectionId,
    side,
    strategy
FROM playbooks_learning

VIEW: v_playbooks_norm
CREATE VIEW v_playbooks_norm AS
SELECT
  TRIM(REPLACE(REPLACE(marketId,'TEST.',''),'TEST-','')) AS marketId_norm,
  CAST(selectionId AS TEXT) AS selectionId_norm,
  *
FROM playbooks

VIEW: v_rank_bin
CREATE VIEW v_rank_bin AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    fav_rank_now AS fav_rank,
    CASE
        WHEN fav_rank_now IN (1,2) THEN '1_2'
        WHEN fav_rank_now BETWEEN 3 AND 5 THEN '3_5'
        WHEN fav_rank_now BETWEEN 6 AND 9 THEN '6_9'
        ELSE '10_plus'
    END AS fav_rank_bin
FROM odds_current
WHERE fav_rank_now IS NOT NULL

VIEW: v_runner_anchor_off
CREATE VIEW v_runner_anchor_off AS
WITH anchor AS (
    SELECT marketId, selectionId, odd AS anchor_odds
    FROM oc_series
    WHERE stage='OC0'
),
off AS (
    SELECT marketId, selectionId, odd AS off_odds
    FROM oc_series
    WHERE stage='OC6'  -- replace with confirmed off-stage if different
)
SELECT
    a.marketId,
    a.selectionId,
    a.anchor_odds,
    o.off_odds,
    ROUND((o.off_odds - a.anchor_odds) / NULLIF(a.anchor_odds,0),4) AS drift_ratio
FROM anchor a
JOIN off o USING (marketId, selectionId)

VIEW: v_runner_context_form
CREATE VIEW v_runner_context_form AS
WITH odds_band AS (
  SELECT
      dr.runner_name,
      r.fav_rank_bin,
      CASE
          WHEN ps.entry_odds <= 1.5 THEN 'below_1_5'
          WHEN ps.entry_odds <= 3   THEN '1_5_to_3'
          WHEN ps.entry_odds <= 5   THEN '3_to_5'
          WHEN ps.entry_odds <= 7   THEN '5_to_7'
          WHEN ps.entry_odds <= 12  THEN '8_to_12'
          ELSE '12_plus'
      END AS odds_band,
      ps.outcome_bin,
      ps.entry_odds
  FROM playbooks_settled ps
  JOIN dashboard_runners dr
       ON ps.marketId = dr.marketId
      AND CAST(ps.selectionId AS TEXT) = dr.selectionId
  LEFT JOIN v_rank_bin r
       ON dr.marketId = r.marketId
      AND dr.selectionId = r.selectionId
)
SELECT
    runner_name,
    fav_rank_bin,
    odds_band,
    COUNT(*)                         AS runs,
    SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN'  THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS wins,
    SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin<>'WIN' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS losses,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN outcome_bin='WIN' THEN 1.0 ELSE 0 END)/COUNT(*),3) AS win_rate,
    ROUND(AVG(entry_odds),2)         AS avg_entry_odds
FROM odds_band
GROUP BY runner_name, fav_rank_bin, odds_band

VIEW: v_runner_form_groups
CREATE VIEW v_runner_form_groups AS
SELECT
    CASE
        WHEN win_rate >= 0.65 THEN 'ELITE'
        WHEN win_rate >= 0.35 THEN 'CONSISTENT'
        WHEN win_rate >= 0.15 THEN 'AVERAGE'
        WHEN win_rate >= 0.05 THEN 'LOW'
        ELSE 'OUTSIDER'
    END AS form_class,
    odds_band,
    fav_rank_bin,
    COUNT(*) AS horses,
    ROUND(AVG(win_rate),3)  AS avg_win_rate,
    ROUND(AVG(avg_entry_odds),2) AS avg_odds
FROM v_runner_context_form
GROUP BY form_class, odds_band, fav_rank_bin

VIEW: v_runner_trading_zones
CREATE VIEW v_runner_trading_zones AS
SELECT
    runner_name,
    fav_rank_bin,
    odds_band,
    CASE
        WHEN odds_band='below_1_5' THEN 'short_fav'
        WHEN odds_band IN ('1_5_to_3','3_to_5','5_to_7') THEN 'active_zone'
        WHEN odds_band='8_to_12' THEN 'passive_zone'
        ELSE 'non_trade_zone'
    END AS trading_zone,
    runs,
    wins,
    losses,
    win_rate,
    avg_entry_odds
FROM v_runner_context_form

VIEW: v_runner_truth
CREATE VIEW v_runner_truth AS
SELECT
    day,
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    net_pl,
    CASE WHEN net_pl > 0 THEN 'PROFIT' ELSE 'LOSS' END AS trade_result,
    outcome_bin
FROM playbooks_settled

VIEW: v_runner_zone_transitions
CREATE VIEW v_runner_zone_transitions AS
SELECT
    a.marketId,
    a.selectionId,
    dr.runner_name,
    r.fav_rank_bin,
    a.anchor_odds,
    a.off_odds,
    ROUND((a.off_odds - a.anchor_odds)/NULLIF(a.anchor_odds,0),4) AS drift_ratio,
    CASE
        WHEN a.anchor_odds > 12  AND a.off_odds <= 12 THEN 'entered_passive'
        WHEN a.anchor_odds > 8   AND a.off_odds <= 8  THEN 'entered_active'
        WHEN a.anchor_odds <= 8  AND a.off_odds > 12 THEN 'exited_active'
        WHEN a.anchor_odds <= 12 AND a.off_odds > 12 THEN 'exited_passive'
        ELSE 'no_change'
    END AS zone_transition
FROM v_runner_anchor_off a
LEFT JOIN dashboard_runners dr
       ON a.marketId = dr.marketId
      AND CAST(a.selectionId AS TEXT) = dr.selectionId
LEFT JOIN v_rank_bin r
       ON dr.marketId = r.marketId
      AND dr.selectionId = r.selectionId

VIEW: v_strategy_perf
CREATE VIEW v_strategy_perf AS
        WITH base AS (
          SELECT
            id, role, hedge_of,
            date(COALESCE(opened_at,ts))                 AS d,
            UPPER(COALESCE(entry_status,''))             AS es,
            UPPER(COALESCE(exit_status ,''))             AS xs,
            UPPER(COALESCE(side,''))                     AS side,
            COALESCE(entry_matched_odds,  entry_odds,  0.0) AS odds,
            COALESCE(entry_matched_stake, entry_stake, 0.0) AS stake,
            COALESCE(mode,'')                            AS mode,
            CASE WHEN COALESCE(source,'')<>'' THEN UPPER(SUBSTR(source,1,1)) ELSE 'S' END AS letter,
            CASE
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='A' THEN 'ALWAYS_ON'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='B' THEN 'BTL_SCOUT'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='G' THEN 'BTL_AGGR'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='X' THEN 'S4_CROSSOVER'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='R' THEN 'S5_BREAKOUT'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='F' THEN 'S6_STEAM_FADE'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='M' THEN 'LADDER_STRATEGY'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='Z' THEN 'OG_BIAS'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='I' THEN 'IP1_SHOCK_DRIFT'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='T' THEN 'IP2_TIRED_LEADER'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='C' THEN 'IP3_CLOSE_FINISH'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='E' THEN 'IP4_FENCE_ERROR'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='K' THEN 'IP5_COLLAPSE_FADE'
              WHEN UPPER(SUBSTR(COALESCE(source,''),1,1))='S' THEN 'LEGACY_S'
              ELSE 'UNKNOWN'
            END AS strategy_name,
            COALESCE(net_pl, realized_pnl, 0.0)          AS pnl,
            COALESCE(opened_at,ts)                       AS opened_ts,
            COALESCE(closed_at,opened_at,ts)             AS closed_ts
          FROM orders
          WHERE date(COALESCE(opened_at,ts)) = date('now')  -- today-only (unchanged)
        ),
        mode_sel AS (SELECT * FROM base WHERE UPPER(mode)=UPPER('TEST') ),
        open_parents AS (
          SELECT id FROM mode_sel
           WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT')
             AND es='MATCHED' AND xs<>'MATCHED'
        ),
        liab_unmatched AS (
          SELECT letter,
                 SUM(CASE WHEN side='LAY' THEN (odds-1.0)*stake ELSE stake END) AS v
          FROM mode_sel
          WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT') AND es IN ('QUEUED','PLACED')
          GROUP BY letter
        ),
        liab_matched_open AS (
          SELECT letter,
                 SUM(CASE WHEN side='LAY' THEN (odds-1.0)*stake ELSE stake END) AS v
          FROM mode_sel
          WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT') AND es='MATCHED' AND xs NOT IN ('MATCHED','SETTLED')
          GROUP BY letter
        ),
        pnl_today AS (
          SELECT letter, ROUND(SUM(pnl),2) AS pnl
          FROM mode_sel
          WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT') AND xs='MATCHED'
          GROUP BY letter
        ),
        last_trade AS (
          SELECT letter, MAX(opened_ts) AS last_ts
          FROM mode_sel WHERE (role IS NULL OR role='PARENT')
          GROUP BY letter
        )
        SELECT
          COALESCE(bl.strategy_name, bl.letter) AS strategy,
          SUM((bl.role IS NULL OR bl.role='PARENT') AND bl.es='QUEUED')                                  AS p_q,
          SUM((bl.role IS NULL OR bl.role='PARENT') AND bl.es='PLACED')                                  AS p_p,
          SUM((bl.role IS NULL OR bl.role='PARENT') AND bl.es='MATCHED')                                 AS p_m,
          SUM(UPPER(COALESCE(bl.role,''))='CHILD' AND bl.es='QUEUED')                                    AS c_q,
          SUM(UPPER(COALESCE(bl.role,''))='CHILD' AND bl.es='PLACED')                                    AS c_p,
          SUM(UPPER(COALESCE(bl.role,''))='CHILD' AND (bl.es='MATCHED' OR bl.xs='MATCHED'))              AS c_m,
          (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM open_parents op JOIN mode_sel b2 ON b2.id=op.id AND b2.letter=bl.letter) AS open_parents,
          ROUND(COALESCE((SELECT v FROM liab_matched_open WHERE letter=bl.letter),0),2)                  AS matched_liab,
          ROUND(COALESCE((SELECT v FROM liab_unmatched   WHERE letter=bl.letter),0),2)                    AS unmatched_liab,
          ROUND(COALESCE((SELECT pnl FROM pnl_today      WHERE letter=bl.letter),0),2)                    AS pnl_today,
          COALESCE((SELECT last_ts FROM last_trade       WHERE letter=bl.letter),'')                      AS last_trade_ts
        FROM mode_sel bl
        GROUP BY bl.letter, bl.strategy_name
        ORDER BY strategy

VIEW: v_timing_features_train_v7
CREATE VIEW v_timing_features_train_v7 AS
SELECT
    p.marketId,
    p.selectionId,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='PRE' THEN p.avg_odds END)        AS pre_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.avg_odds END)     AS inplay_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='POST' THEN p.avg_odds END)       AS post_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='PRE' THEN p.rel_volatility END)  AS pre_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.rel_volatility END) AS inplay_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='POST' THEN p.rel_volatility END) AS post_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.odds_rate_per_min END) AS drift_speed,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.avg_progress_ratio END) AS inplay_progress,
    ROUND(
        MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.phase_end_min - p.phase_start_min END),
        1
    ) AS expected_race_mins
FROM v_phase_performance p
GROUP BY p.marketId, p.selectionId

VIEW: v_timing_features_v7
CREATE VIEW v_timing_features_v7 AS
SELECT
    p.marketId,
    p.selectionId,
    p.surface_type,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='PRE' THEN p.avg_odds END) AS pre_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.avg_odds END) AS inplay_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='POST' THEN p.avg_odds END) AS post_avg_odds,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='PRE' THEN p.rel_volatility END) AS pre_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.rel_volatility END) AS inplay_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='POST' THEN p.rel_volatility END) AS post_vol,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.odds_rate_per_min END) AS drift_speed,
    MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.avg_progress_ratio END) AS inplay_progress,
    ROUND(
        MAX(CASE WHEN p.phase='INPLAY' THEN p.phase_end_min - p.phase_start_min END),
        1
    ) AS expected_race_mins
FROM v_phase_performance p
GROUP BY p.marketId, p.selectionId

VIEW: v_trading_zone_groups
CREATE VIEW v_trading_zone_groups AS
SELECT
    CASE
        WHEN odds_band='below_1_5' THEN 'short_fav'
        WHEN odds_band IN ('1_5_to_3','3_to_5','5_to_7') THEN 'active_zone'
        WHEN odds_band='8_to_12' THEN 'passive_zone'
        ELSE 'non_trade_zone'
    END AS trading_zone,
    CASE
        WHEN odds_band='below_1_5' THEN 'short_priced_favourite'
        WHEN odds_band='1_5_to_3'  THEN 'strong_favourite'
        WHEN odds_band='3_to_5'    THEN 'moderate_favourite'
        WHEN odds_band='5_to_7'    THEN 'weak_favourite'
        WHEN odds_band='8_to_12'   THEN 'passive_zone'
        ELSE 'outsider'
    END AS strength_label,
    odds_band,
    COUNT(*)                AS horses,
    ROUND(AVG(win_rate),3)  AS avg_win_rate,
    ROUND(AVG(avg_entry_odds),2) AS avg_odds
FROM v_runner_context_form
GROUP BY trading_zone, strength_label, odds_band

VIEW: v_weight_of_money
CREATE VIEW v_weight_of_money AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    ROUND(
        CASE 
            WHEN (back1 + lay1) != 0 THEN (lay1 - back1) / (lay1 + back1)
            ELSE 0.0
        END, 4
    ) AS wom_ratio,
    CASE
        WHEN (lay1 - back1) / (lay1 + back1) >  0.05 THEN 'LAY_DOMINANT'
        WHEN (lay1 - back1) / (lay1 + back1) < -0.05 THEN 'BACK_DOMINANT'
        ELSE 'BALANCED'
    END AS wom_state
FROM odds_current
WHERE back1 IS NOT NULL AND lay1 IS NOT NULL

VIEW: volatility_meta
CREATE VIEW volatility_meta AS
WITH prev AS (
  SELECT
      a.marketId,
      a.selectionId,
      a.id AS id_a,
      MAX(b.id) AS id_prev
  FROM oc_series a
  JOIN oc_series b
    ON a.marketId=b.marketId
   AND a.selectionId=b.selectionId
   AND b.id < a.id
  GROUP BY a.marketId, a.selectionId, a.id
),
diffs AS (
  SELECT
      a.marketId,
      a.selectionId,
      ABS(a.odd - b.odd)/a.odd AS rel_change
  FROM oc_series a
  JOIN prev p  ON a.id=p.id_a
  JOIN oc_series b ON b.id=p.id_prev
  WHERE a.odd IS NOT NULL AND b.odd IS NOT NULL
)
SELECT
    marketId,
    selectionId,
    ROUND(AVG(rel_change),4) AS avg_volatility,
    MIN(a.odd) AS range_low,
    MAX(a.odd) AS range_high
FROM oc_series a
LEFT JOIN diffs d USING(marketId,selectionId)
GROUP BY marketId,selectionId

VIEWS:
  v_settle_day
  v_settle_mkt_day

VIEW: v_settle_day
CREATE VIEW v_settle_day AS
SELECT day, SUM(net) AS net, COUNT(*) AS mkts
FROM v_settle_mkt_day
GROUP BY 1

VIEW: v_settle_mkt_day
CREATE VIEW v_settle_mkt_day AS
SELECT date(datetime(settledDate,'utc')) AS day,
       marketId,
       SUM(profit) AS net,
       COUNT(*) AS bets
FROM bf_cleared_orders
WHERE settledDate IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY 1,2

VIEWS:
  v7_mastery_race_clusters_new
  v7_mastery_race_summary_new
  v7_mastery_race_view_new

VIEW: v7_mastery_race_clusters_new
CREATE VIEW v7_mastery_race_clusters_new AS
SELECT
    v.marketId,
    v.selectionId,
    CASE
        WHEN v.drift_speed < -0.10 THEN 'FAST_STEAMER'
        WHEN v.drift_speed BETWEEN -0.10 AND 0.05 THEN 'STEADY'
        WHEN v.drift_speed > 0.05 THEN 'DRIFTER'
        ELSE 'UNKNOWN'
    END AS narrative_cluster,
    ROUND(1.0/NULLIF(v.anchor_odd,0),4) AS preoff_win_pct,
    v.win_rate,
    v.form_avg_pnl
FROM v7_mastery_race_view_new AS v

VIEW: v7_mastery_race_summary_new
CREATE VIEW v7_mastery_race_summary_new AS
SELECT
    marketId,
    COUNT(DISTINCT selectionId) AS runners,
    ROUND(AVG(drift_speed),4) AS avg_drift_speed,
    ROUND(AVG(inplay_progress),4) AS avg_progress,
    ROUND(AVG(expected_race_mins),2) AS avg_expected_mins,
    ROUND(SUM(CASE WHEN success_flag=1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END)*1.0/COUNT(*),3) AS win_rate_est,
    datetime('now','utc') AS created_at
FROM story_enriched_cache
GROUP BY marketId
/* v7_mastery_race_summary_new(marketId,runners,avg_drift_speed,avg_progress,avg_expected_mins,win_rate_est,created_at) */

VIEW: v7_mastery_race_view_new
CREATE VIEW v7_mastery_race_view_new AS
SELECT
    m.marketId,
    m.selectionId,
    'Stability / Goal Alignment' AS bucket,
    m.net_pl AS pnl,
    CASE WHEN m.net_pl > 0 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS success,
    ABS(m.drift_speed) AS coherence,
    m.drift_speed,
    m.inplay_progress,
    m.expected_race_mins,
    m.win_rate,
    m.form_avg_pnl,
    0.5 AS pre_conf,
    0.5 AS inplay_conf,
    0.0 AS story_divergence,
    'NEUTRAL' AS momentum_class,
    'STEADY'  AS race_pattern,
    datetime('now','utc') AS created_at
FROM cache_mastery_day AS m
WHERE m.marketId IS NOT NULL AND m.selectionId IS NOT NULL
/* v7_mastery_race_view_new(marketId,selectionId,bucket,pnl,success,coherence,drift_speed,inplay_progress,expected_race_mins,win_rate,form_avg_pnl,pre_conf,inplay_conf,story_divergence,momentum_class,race_pattern,created_at) */
